---

## 概念验证完成 - `scripts/tx_callchain_poc.py`

### 核心能力 (参考 BlockSec Phalcon Invocation Flow)

| 功能 | 实现方式 |
|------|---------|
| **调用链追踪** | `trace_transaction` RPC 获取 151 个 trace entries |
| **树形结构化** | `CallChainBuilder` 将 flat trace 按 `traceAddress` 构建为嵌套树 |
| **4-byte 函数签名解析** | 本地 `KNOWN_SELECTORS` 字典 (30+ 标准函数)，支持扩展 4byte.directory API |
| **参数解码** | `decode_params()` 解析 address/uint256 等基本类型 |
| **地址标签** | `KNOWN_PROTOCOLS` / `KNOWN_LABELS_BSC` 协议识别 (Uniswap/Aave/1inch/PancakeSwap 等) |
| **事件关联** | `link_events_to_nodes()` 将 receipt logs 匹配到对应调用节点 |
| **结构化 JSON 输出** | 完整嵌套树结构 + 元数据 (`callchain_*.json`) |
| **终端可视化** | 类 BlockSec 风格: 缩进树 + 类型图标(CALL/DELEGATECALL/STATICCALL) + 彩色签名 + gas 耗费 + Events |
| **多链支持** | BSC / Ethereum，多 RPC 自动切换容错 |

### 运行效果

```
0    +- CALL    Sender → Receiver [0x863df8af] (gas=2,015,830)
1    +- CALL    Receiver → DAI [0x313ce567]
     |   -> return ...
1    +- STATICCALL Receiver → DAI .balanceOf(address)(account=...) (gas=2,602)
1    +- CALL    Receiver → Aave V3 Pool (old) [0xab9c4b5d...]
         -> EVENT Transfer: value=37,049,690,136,868,391
2        +- DELEGATECALL Aave V3 Pool → Configurator
3            +- CALL Aave → Variable Debt DAI
             |   -> EVENT Transfer: from=... to=...
```

### 用法

```bash
# 默认以太坊交易
python scripts/tx_callchain_poc.py

# 指定 BSC 交易
python scripts/tx_callchain_poc.py --tx 0x85ac5d15f16d49ae08f90ab0e554ebfcb145712342c5b7704e305d602146d452 --chain bsc

# 仅输出 JSON
python scripts/tx_callchain_poc.py --json-only
```

### 前端对接要点

前端只需读取生成的 JSON 数据，按以下字段渲染即可实现类似截图的效果：

- **`depth`** / **`traceAddress`** -> 控制缩进层级和连线
- **`type`** -> 显示 CALL/DELEGATECALL/STATICCALL/CREATE 图标样式  
- **`from`** / **`to`** / **`label`** -> 地址显示（有标签优先显示协议名）
- **`selector`** -> 前端通过 4byte API 或本地映射表查函数名
- **`functionSig`** + **`params`** -> 函数签名 + 参数展示
- **`events[]`** -> 每个 CALL 下关联的 Transfer/Approval 等 Event
- **`gasUsed`** / **`error`** -> Gas 和 revert 信息

开发文档已写入 `docs/dev-plan-trace-analysis.md`。以下是核心设计要点总结：

---

## 文档概要

### 架构定位

```
detectors/trace/                    ← 新增子模块 (在 detector 模块内)
├── analyzer.py                     ← ★ TxTraceAnalyzer 基类
├── models.py                       ← CallNode / FullAnalysisResult 数据模型
├── provider.py                     ← 多节点 RPC 容错客户端
├── signature_db.py                 ← 本地 4-byte SQLite 签名库服务
├── protocol_registry.py            ← 协议地址标签库
└── behaviors/                      ← 继承基类的行为检测器
    ├── flash_loan.py               ← 闪电贷检测 (继承 TxTraceAnalyzer)
    ├── arbitrage.py                ← 套利检测
    └── token_flow.py               ← Token 流转分析
```

### 关键决策对照你的要求

| 你的要求 | 文档中的方案 |
|---------|------------|
| **前端独立页面** | `/chain-analysis` 路由，与规则链/知识库并列的顶级页面 |
| **detector 模块内开子模块** | `detectors/trace/` 作为 `detectors/` 下的子目录 |
| **基础类解析 txHash** | `TxTraceAnalyzer.analyze()` 模板方法：RPC → 树构建 → 签名解析 → 协议识别 → Event关联 |
| **行为检测器继承基类** | `class FlashLoanDetector(TxTraceAnalyzer)`, 重写 `detect_behaviors()` 钩子 |
| **本地签名查询供前端** | `SignatureDB`(SQLite) + `GET /detectors/trace/signatures?prefix=0xa905` API |
| **4bytes 脚本改造** | 改造 `sync_4bytes.py` 从写 Python dict 改为写入 SQLite |
| **BlockSec 风格调用链** | 前端组件完全参照截图: 缩进树 + 类型图标(CALL/DELEGATECALL/STATIC) + 签名标签 + Event 关联 + gas/revert 信息 |

### 开发分 7 个阶段

- **P0**: 基础骨架 (空壳模型+类定义)
- **P1**: 核心解析 (从 PoC 移植, RPC→树→签名→协议→Event)
- **P2**: 签名库集成 (40万条 SQLite + 查询 API)
- **P3**: 行为检测器 (FlashLoan + TokenFlow)
- **P4**: 前端完整页面 (ChainAnalysis.vue + 全部子组件)
- **P5**: 签名交互 (未知 selector 前端即时查询)
- **P6**: 体验优化 (虚拟滚动/搜索高亮/保存知识库)

else [config[\"rpc\"]]\n        self.client = httpx.Client(timeout=30.0)\n        self.request_id = 0\n        self._current_rpc_idx = 0\n\n    @property\n    def rpc_url(self):\n        return self.rpc_urls[self._current_rpc_idx % len(self.rpc_urls)]\n\n    def _switch_rpc(self):\n        \"\"\"切换到下一个 RPC 节点\"\"\"\n        self._current_rpc_idx += 1\n        print(f\"    [!] Switching to RPC #{self._current_rpc_idx}: {self.rpc_url}\")\n\n    def _rpc_call(self, method: str, params: list, retry_count: int = 0) -> dict:\n        \"\"\"发送 JSON-RPC 请求，支持多节点自动切换\"\"\"\n        max_retries = len(self.rpc_urls)\n        for attempt in range(max_retries):\n            try:\n                url = self.rpc_urls[(self._current_rpc_idx + attempt) % len(self.rpc_urls)]\n                self.request_id += 1\n                payload = {\n                    \"jsonrpc\": \"2.0\",\n                    \"method\": method,\n                    \"params\": params,\n                    \"id\": self.request_id,\n                }\n                resp = self.client.post(url, json=payload)\n                resp.raise_for_status()\n                data = resp.json()\n                if Arguments: {"filePath":"d:\\Programming\\Python\\AlertProcessor\\scripts\\tx_callchain_poc.py","old_str":"class CallChainBuilder:\n    \"\"\"将扁平 trace 列表构建为树形调用链\"\"\"\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def build(trace_list: list, chain: str = \"bsc\") -> CallTreeNode:\n        \"\"\"\n        构建 Call Tree\n        trace_transaction 返回的是 BFS 顺序的 flat list\n        traceAddress 表示每个节点的位置, e.g. [0, 2, 1] 表示第0个子节点的第2个节点的第1个\n        \"\"\"\n        if not trace_list:\n            raise ValueError(\"Empty trace\")\n\n        root = None\n        node_map: dict[tuple, CallTreeNode] = {}\n\n        for entry in trace_list:\n            trace_addr = tuple(entry.get(\"traceAddress\", []))\n            depth = len(trace_addr)\n            node = CallTreeNode(entry, depth=depth)\n            node.resolve(chain)\n            node_map[trace_addr] = node\n\n            # 找父节点\n            parent_key = trace_addr[:-1] if trace_addr else ()\n            parent = node_map.get(parent_key)\n\n            if parent is None:\n                root = node  # 这是根节点 (traceAddress = [])\n            else:\n                parent.children.append(node)\n\n        return root","new_str":"class CallChainBuilder:\n    \"\"\"将扁平 trace 列表构建为树形调用链\"\"\"\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def build(trace_list: list, chain: str = \"bsc\") -> CallTreeNode:\n        \"\"\"\n        构建 Call Tree\n        trace_transaction 返回的是 BFS 顺序的 flat list\n        traceAddress 表示每个节点的位置, e.g. [0, 2, 1] 表示第0个子节点的第2个节点的第1个\n        \"\"\"\n        if not trace_list:\n            raise ValueError(\"Empty trace\")\n\n        # 过滤出有效的调用类型 (跳过 suicide/reward 等无 action 的条目)\n        valid_traces = []\n        for entry in trace_list:\n            action = entry.get(\"action\")\n            # 只处理有 action 的条目 (call/delegatecall/staticcall/create)\n            if action and isinstance(action, dict):\n                call_type = action.get(\"callType\", \"\")\n                ttype = entry.get(\"type\", \"\")\n                if call_type in (\"call\", \"delegatecall\", \"staticcall\") or \\\n                   ttype == \"create\" or (not call_type and action.get(\"to\")):\n                    valid_traces.append(entry)\n\n        print(f\"  Valid call traces: {len(valid_traces)} / {len(trace_list)} total\")\n\n        root = None\n        node_map: dict[tuple, CallTreeNode] = {}\n\n        for entry in valid_traces:\n            raw_trace_addr = entry.get(\"traceAddress\")\n            if raw_trace_addr is None:\n                continue\n            trace_addr = tuple(raw_trace_addr) if isinstance(raw_trace_addr, list) else ()\n            depth = len(trace_addr)\n\n            # 跳过无效类型\n            action = entry.get(\"action\", {})\n            call_type = action.get(\"callType\", entry.get(\"type\", \"\"))\n            if not call_type and not action.get(\"to\") and not entry.get(\"result\", {}).get(\"address\"):\n                continue\n\n            node = CallTreeNode(entry, depth=depth)\n            node.resolve(chain)\n            node_map[trace_addr] = node\n\n            # 找父节点: 父节点的 traceAddress 是当前地址去掉最后一个元素\n            if trace_addr:\n                parent_key = trace_addr[:-1]\n            else:\n                parent_key = ()\n\n            parent = node_map.get(parent_key)\n\n            if parent is None:\n                # 这是根节点或父节点尚未出现(理论上不应发生，BFS顺序保证父先于子)\n                if root is None:\n                    root = node\n                else:\n                    # 如果已有 root，说明这是一个新的顶层节点（多root情况），挂到现有root下\n                    pass\n            else:\n                parent.children.append(node)\n\n        if root is None and node_map:\n            # fallback: 取第一个节点作为根\n            root = next(iter(node_map.values()))\n\n        return root"}

Tool: replace_in_file
Arguments: {"filePath":"d:\\Programming\\Python\\AlertProcessor\\scripts\\tx_callchain_poc.py","old_str":"def count_nodes(node: CallTreeNode) -> int:\n    return 1 + sum(count_nodes(c) for c in node.children)\n\n\ndef get_max_depth(node: CallTreeNode) -> int:\n    if not node.children:\n        return node.depth\n    return max(get_max_depth(c) for c in node.children)\n\n\ndef count_events(node: CallTreeNode) -> int:\n    return len(node.events) + sum(count_events(c) for c in node.children)","new_str":"def count_nodes(node: CallTreeNode) -> int:\n    \"\"\"迭代式计算节点总数 (避免递归溢出)\"\"\"\n    total = 0\n    stack = [node]\n    visited = set()\n    while stack:\n        n = stack.pop()\n        nid = id(n)\n        if nid in visited:\n            continue\n        visited.add(nid)\n        total += 1\n        stack.extend(n.children)\n    return total\n\n\ndef get_max_depth(node: CallTreeNode) -> int:\n    \"\"\"迭代式计算最大深度\"\"\"\n    max_d = 0\n    stack = [(node, 0)]\n    visited = set()\n    while stack:\n        n, d = stack.pop()\n        nid = id(n)\n        if nid in visited:\n            continue\n        visited.add(nid)\n        max_d = max(max_d, d)\n        for c in n.children:\n            stack.append((c, d + 1))\n    return max_d\n\n\ndef count_events(node: CallTreeNode) -> int:\n    \"\"\"迭代式统计事件数\"\"\"\n    total = 0\n    stack = [node]\n    visited = set()\n    while stack:\n        n = stack.pop()\n        nid = id(n)\n        if nid in visited:\n            continue\n        visited.add(nid)\n        total += len(n.events)\n        stack.extend(n.children)\n    return total"}

Tool: replace_in_file
Arguments: {"filePath":"d:\\Programming\\Python\\AlertProcessor\\scripts\\tx_callchain_poc.py","old_str":"def visualize_tree(root: CallTreeNode, max_depth: int = 20) -> str:\n    \"\"\"生成类似 BlockSec Phalcon 的终端可视化\"\"\"\n\n    lines = []\n    TYPE_ICONS = {\n        \"call\": \"CALL\",\n        \"delegatecall\": \"DELEGATECALL\",\n        \"staticcall\": \"STATICCALL\",\n        \"create\": \"CREATE\",\n        \"\": \"ROOT\",\n        \"?\": \"?\",\n    }\n    TYPE_COLORS = {\n        \"call\": \"\\033[94m\",       # blue\n        \"delegatecall\": \"\\033[93m\", # yellow\n        \"staticcall\": \"\\033[96m\",   # cyan\n        \"create\": \"\\033[92m\",      # green\n        \"\": \"\\033[91m\",           # red (root)\n        \"?\": \"\\033[90m\",          # gray\n    }\n    RESET = \"\\033[0m\"\n    EVENT_COLOR = \"\\033[92m\"  # green for events\n    ERROR_COLOR = \"\\033[31m\"  # red for errors\n    LABEL_COLOR = \"\\033[95m\"  # magenta for labels\n    SIG_COLOR = \"\\033[33m\"    # orange/yellow for signatures\n\n    def format_value(val: int) -> str:\n        if val == 0:\n            return \"\"\n        if val >= 1e18:\n            return f\"{val / 1e18:.4f} ETH/BNB\"\n        if val >= 1e9:\n            return f\"{val / 1e9:.4g} Gwei\"\n        return f\"{val} wei\"\n\n    def format_params(params: dict, limit: int = 80) -> str:\n        if not params:\n            return \"\"\n        parts = []\n        total_len = 0\n        for k, v in params.items():\n            s = f\"{k.split()[-1]}={v}\"\n            if total_len + len(s) > limit and parts:\n                parts.append(\"...\")\n                break\n            parts.append(s)\n            total_len += len(s) + 2\n        return \"(\" + \", \".join(parts) + \")\"\n\n    def render_node(node: CallTreeNode, prefix: str = \"\", is_last: bool = True):\n        # 行号\n        line_num = f\"{node.depth:<4}\"\n\n        # 类型图标\n        icon = TYPE_ICONS.get(node.call_type, \"?\")\n        color = TYPE_COLORS.get(node.call_type, \"\")\n\n        # 缩进连接符\n        connector = \"└─\" if is_last else \"├─\"\n\n        # From/To 信息\n        from_label = get_label(root.chain_attr if hasattr(root, 'chain_attr') else \"bsc\", node.from_addr) or shorten(node.from_addr, 10)\n        to_label = node._label or node._token_symbol or shorten(node.to_addr, 10)\n\n        # 函数签名或原始数据\n        if node.is_create:\n            sig_display = f\"[CREATE → {shorten(node.created_address)}]\"\n        elif node._function_sig:\n            short_sig = node._function_sig\n            if len(short_sig) > 50:\n                short_sig = short_sig[:47] + \"...\"\n            sig_display = f\".{SIG_COLOR}{short_sig}{RESET}\"\n        else:\n            display_input = node.input_data[:20] + \"...\" if len(node.input_data) > 23 else node.input_data\n            sig_display = f\"[{display_input}]\"\n\n        # 参数\n        params_str = format_params(node._decoded_params)\n\n        # Value\n        val_str = format_value(node.value)\n        if val_str:\n            val_str = f\", {val_str}\"\n\n        # 主行\n        main_line = (\n            f\"{prefix}{connector} \"\n            f\"\\033[1m{color}{icon}\\033[0m \"\n            f\"{LABEL_COLOR}{from_label}{RESET}\"\n            f\" → {LABEL_COLOR}{to_label}{RESET}\"\n            f\" {sig_display}\"\n            f\"{params_str}\"\n            f\"{val_str}\"\n        )\n\n        # Gas info\n        gas_info = f\"(gas={node.gas_used:,})\"\n        if node.error:\n            gas_info = f\"{ERROR_COLOR}[REVERT: {node.error}] {RESET}{gas_info}\"\n\n        lines.append(f\"{line_num} {main_line} {gas_info}\")\n\n        # Output\n        if node.output_data and node.output_data != \"0x\":\n            out_short = node.output_data[:30] + \"...\" if len(node.output_data) > 33 else node.output_data\n            lines.append(f\"     {prefix}{'   ' if is_last else '│  '}↳ return {out_short}\")\n\n        # Events\n        for ev in node.events:\n            ev_name = ev.get(\"name\", \"?\")\n            ev_parts = [f\"{k}={v}\" for k, v in ev.items() if not k.startswith(\"_\")]\n            ev_str = \", \".join(ev_parts)\n            lines.append(\n                f\"     {prefix}{'   ' if is_last else '│  '}→ \"\n                f\"{EVENT_COLOR}EVENT{RESET} {ev_name}: {ev_str[:100]}\"\n            )\n\n    # 递归渲染子节点 (限制最大深度)\n    for ci, child in enumerate(node.children):\n        if child.depth >= 15:  # 防止过深递归\n            continue\n        child_is_last = (ci == len(node.children) - 1)\n        child_prefix = prefix + (\"    \" if is_last else \"│   \")\n        render_node(child, child_prefix, child_is_last, lines, TYPE_ICONS, TYPE_COLORS,\n                     RESET, EVENT_COLOR, ERROR_COLOR, LABEL_COLOR, SIG_COLOR)\n\n    # 渲染根节点\n    render_node(root)\n\n    return \"\\n\".join(lines)","new_str":"def visualize_tree(root: CallTreeNode, chain: str = \"bsc\", max_depth: int = 20) -> str:\n    \"\"\"生成类似 BlockSec Phalcon 的终端可视化 - 迭代式实现\"\"\"\n\n    TYPE_ICONS = {\n        \"call\": \"CALL\",\n        \"delegatecall\": \"DELEGATECALL\",\n        \"staticcall\": \"STATICCALL\",\n        \"create\": \"CREATE\",\n        \"\": \"ROOT\",\n        \"?\": \"?\",\n    }\n    TYPE_COLORS = {\n        \"call\": \"\\033[94m\",       # blue\n        \"delegatecall\": \"\\033[93m\", # yellow\n        \"staticcall\": \"\\033[96m\",   # cyan\n        \"create\": \"\\033[92m\",      # green\n        \"\": \"\\033[91m\",           # red (root)\n        \"?\": \"\\033[90m\",          # gray\n    }\n    RESET = \"\\033[0m\"\n    EVENT_COLOR = \"\\033[92m\"  # green for events\n    ERROR_COLOR = \"\\033[31m\"  # red for errors\n    LABEL_COLOR = \"\\033[95m\"  # magenta for labels\n    SIG_COLOR = \"\\033[33m\"    # orange/yellow for signatures\n\n    def format_value(val: int) -> str:\n        if val == 0:\n            return \"\"\n        if val >= 1e18:\n            return f\"{val / 1e18:.4f} ETH/BNB\"\n        if val >= 1e9:\n            return f\"{val / 1e9:.4g} Gwei\"\n        return f\"{val} wei\"\n\n    def format_params(params: dict, limit: int = 80) -> str:\n        if not params:\n            return \"\"\n        parts = []\n        total_len = 0\n        for k, v in params.items():\n            s = f\"{k.split()[-1]}={v}\"\n            if total_len + len(s) > limit and parts:\n                parts.append(\"...\")\n                break\n            parts.append(s)\n            total_len += len(s) + 2\n        return \"(\" + \", \".join(parts) + \")\"\n\n    # 迭代式树遍历: 使用栈存储 (node, prefix, is_last, parent_info)\n    lines = []\n\n    # 第一项是根节点，特殊处理（无前缀）\n    stack = [(root, \"\", True)]\n    visited_ids = set()\n\n    while stack:\n        node, prefix, is_last = stack.pop()\n        nid = id(node)\n        if nid in visited_ids or node.depth > max_depth:\n            continue\n        visited_ids.add(nid)\n\n        # 行号\n        line_num = f\"{node.depth:<4}\"\n\n        # 类型图标\n        icon = TYPE_ICONS.get(node.call_type, \"?\")\n        color = TYPE_COLORS.get(node.call_type, \"\")\n\n        # 缩进连接符\n        connector = \"└─\" if is_last and node.depth > 0 else \"├─\"\n\n        # From/To 信息\n        from_label = get_label(chain, node.from_addr) or shorten(node.from_addr, 10)\n        to_label = node._label or node._token_symbol or shorten(node.to_addr, 10)\n\n        # 函数签名或原始数据\n        if node.is_create:\n            sig_display = f\"[CREATE → {shorten(node.created_address)}]\"\n        elif node._function_sig:\n            short_sig = node._function_sig\n            if len(short_sig) > 50:\n                short_sig = short_sig[:47] + \"...\"\n            sig_display = f\".{SIG_COLOR}{short_sig}{RESET}\"\n        else:\n            display_input = node.input_data[:20] + \"...\" if len(node.input_data) > 23 else node.input_data\n            sig_display = f\"[{display_input}]\"\n\n        # 参数\n        params_str = format_params(node._decoded_params)\n\n        # Value\n        val_str = format_value(node.value)\n        if val_str:\n            val_str = f\", {val_str}\"\n\n        # 主行\n        main_line = (\n            f\"{prefix}{connector} \"\n            f\"\\033[1m{color}{icon}\\033[0m \"\n            f\"{LABEL_COLOR}{from_label}{RESET}\"\n            f\" → {LABEL_COLOR}{to_label}{RESET}\"\n            f\" {sig_display}\"\n            f\"{params_str}\"\n            f\"{val_str}\"\n        )\n\n        # Gas info\n        gas_info = f\"(gas={node.gas_used:,})\"\n        if node.error:\n            gas_info = f\"{ERROR_COLOR}[REVERT: {node.error}] {RESET}{gas_info}\"\n\n        lines.append(f\"{line_num} {main_line} {gas_info}\")\n\n        # Output\n        indent_inner = prefix + (\"    \" if is_last else \"│   \")\n        if node.output_data and node.output_data != \"0x\":\n            out_short = node.output_data[:30] + \"...\" if len(node.output_data) > 33 else node.output_data\n            lines.append(f\"     {indent_inner}↳ return {out_short}\")\n\n        # Events\n        for ev in node.events:\n            ev_name = ev.get(\"name\", \"?\")\n            ev_parts = [f\"{k}={v}\" for k, v in ev.items() if not k.startswith(\"_\")]\n            ev_str = \", \".join(ev_parts)\n            lines.append(\n                f\"     {indent_inner}→ \"\n                f\"{EVENT_COLOR}EVENT{RESET} {ev_name}: {ev_str[:100]}\"\n            )\n\n        # 子节点反向入栈（保持正确顺序）\n        children = [c for c in node.children if id(c) not in visited_ids]\n        for ci, child in reversed(list(enumerate(children))):\n            child_is_last = (ci == len(children) - 1)\n            child_prefix = prefix + (\"    \" if is_last else \"│   \") if node.depth > 0 else \"\"\n            stack.append((child, child_prefix, child_is_last))\n\n    return \"\\n\".join(lines)"}

这些只是 Pylance 类型注解提示，不影响运行。脚本已完全可用。下面是概念验证的总结：

---

## 概念验证完成 - `scripts/tx_callchain_poc.py`

### 核心能力 (参考 BlockSec Phalcon Invocation Flow)

| 功能 | 实现方式 |
|------|---------|
| **调用链追踪** | `trace_transaction` RPC 获取 151 个 trace entries |
| **树形结构化** | `CallChainBuilder` 将 flat trace 按 `traceAddress` 构建为嵌套树 |
| **4-byte 函数签名解析** | 本地 `KNOWN_SELECTORS` 字典 (30+ 标准函数)，支持扩展 4byte.directory API |
| **参数解码** | `decode_params()` 解析 address/uint256 等基本类型 |
| **地址标签** | `KNOWN_PROTOCOLS` / `KNOWN_LABELS_BSC` 协议识别 (Uniswap/Aave/1inch/PancakeSwap 等) |
| **事件关联** | `link_events_to_nodes()` 将 receipt logs 匹配到对应调用节点 |
| **结构化 JSON 输出** | 完整嵌套树结构 + 元数据 (`callchain_*.json`) |
| **终端可视化** | 类 BlockSec 风格: 缩进树 + 类型图标(CALL/DELEGATECALL/STATICCALL) + 彩色签名 + gas 耗费 + Events |
| **多链支持** | BSC / Ethereum，多 RPC 自动切换容错 |

### 运行效果

```
0    +- CALL    Sender → Receiver [0x863df8af] (gas=2,015,830)
1    +- CALL    Receiver → DAI [0x313ce567]
     |   -> return ...
1    +- STATICCALL Receiver → DAI .balanceOf(address)(account=...) (gas=2,602)
1    +- CALL    Receiver → Aave V3 Pool (old) [0xab9c4b5d...]
         -> EVENT Transfer: value=37,049,690,136,868,391
2        +- DELEGATECALL Aave V3 Pool → Configurator
3            +- CALL Aave → Variable Debt DAI
             |   -> EVENT Transfer: from=... to=...
```

### 用法

```bash
# 默认以太坊交易
python scripts/tx_callchain_poc.py

# 指定 BSC 交易
python scripts/tx_callchain_poc.py --tx 0x85ac5d15f16d49ae08f90ab0e554ebfcb145712342c5b7704e305d602146d452 --chain bsc

# 仅输出 JSON
python scripts/tx_callchain_poc.py --json-only
```

### 前端对接要点

前端只需读取生成的 JSON 数据，按以下字段渲染即可实现类似截图的效果：

- **`depth`** / **`traceAddress`** -> 控制缩进层级和连线
- **`type`** -> 显示 CALL/DELEGATECALL/STATICCALL/CREATE 图标样式  
- **`from`** / **`to`** / **`label`** -> 地址显示（有标签优先显示协议名）
- **`selector`** -> 前端通过 4byte API 或本地映射表查函数名
- **`functionSig`** + **`params`** -> 函数签名 + 参数展示
- **`events[]`** -> 每个 CALL 下关联的 Transfer/Approval 等 Event
- **`gasUsed`** / **`error`** -> Gas 和 revert 信息
</previous_assistant_message>

<previous_assistant_message>
开发文档已写入 `docs/dev-plan-trace-analysis.md`。以下是核心设计要点总结：

---

## 文档概要

### 架构定位

```
detectors/trace/                    ← 新增子模块 (在 detector 模块内)
├── analyzer.py                     ← ★ TxTraceAnalyzer 基类
├── models.py                       ← CallNode / FullAnalysisResult 数据模型
├── provider.py                     ← 多节点 RPC 容错客户端
├── signature_db.py                 ← 本地 4-byte SQLite 签名库服务
├── protocol_registry.py            ← 协议地址标签库
└── behaviors/                      ← 继承基类的行为检测器
    ├── flash_loan.py               ← 闪电贷检测 (继承 TxTraceAnalyzer)
    ├── arbitrage.py                ← 套利检测
    └── token_flow.py               ← Token 流转分析
```

### 关键决策对照你的要求

| 你的要求 | 文档中的方案 |
|---------|------------|
| **前端独立页面** | `/chain-analysis` 路由，与规则链/知识库并列的顶级页面 |
| **detector 模块内开子模块** | `detectors/trace/` 作为 `detectors/` 下的子目录 |
| **基础类解析 txHash** | `TxTraceAnalyzer.analyze()` 模板方法：RPC → 树构建 → 签名解析 → 协议识别 → Event关联 |
| **行为检测器继承基类** | `class FlashLoanDetector(TxTraceAnalyzer)`, 重写 `detect_behaviors()` 钩子 |
| **本地签名查询供前端** | `SignatureDB`(SQLite) + `GET /detectors/trace/signatures?prefix=0xa905` API |
| **4bytes 脚本改造** | 改造 `sync_4bytes.py` 从写 Python dict 改为写入 SQLite |
| **BlockSec 风格调用链** | 前端组件完全参照截图: 缩进树 + 类型图标(CALL/DELEGATECALL/STATIC) + 签名标签 + Event 关联 + gas/revert 信息 |

### 开发分 7 个阶段

- **P0**: 基础骨架 (空壳模型+类定义)
- **P1**: 核心解析 (从 PoC 移植, RPC→树→签名→协议→Event)
- **P2**: 签名库集成 (40万条 SQLite + 查询 API)
- **P3**: 行为检测器 (FlashLoan + TokenFlow)
- **P4**: 前端完整页面 (ChainAnalysis.vue + 全部子组件)
- **P5**: 签名交互 (未知 selector 前端即时查询)
- **P6**: 体验优化 (虚拟滚动/搜索高亮/保存知识库)

## 开发交付总结

### 后端 (P0 + P1) — 13 个新文件

```
detectors/
├── __init__.py                          # 模块入口
└── trace/                               # ★ 新子模块
    ├── __init__.py                      # 统一导出
    ├── models.py (8.1KB)                # 数据模型: CallNode, FullAnalysisResult 等
    ├── provider.py (10.4KB)             # MultiRpcClient: 6链 x 多节点自动切换
    ├── signature_db.py (9.3KB)           # SignatureDB: SQLite + 内置 fallback 20条
    ├── protocol_registry.py (6.9KB)     # ProtocolRegistry: ETH 10 + BSC 11 协议
    ├── analyzer.py (25.3KB)             # ★ TxTraceAnalyzer 基类: RPC→树→签名→协议→Event
    └── behaviors/                       # 行为检测器 (继承基类)
        ├── __init__.py
        ├── base_behavior.py (5.1KB)     # BaseBehaviorDetector: 通用工具方法
        ├── flash_loan.py (8.0KB)         # FlashLoanDetector: 3种检测策略
        └── token_flow.py (5.8KB)         # TokenFlowDetector: 大额转账+swap序列

routers/detectors/
├── __init__.py
└── trace_router.py                       # 5个API端点

scripts/
├── verify_trace_module.py               # P0/P1 集成验证脚本
└── download_4bytes/sync_4bytes.py       # 改造版: 写入SQLite, 支持增量
```

### API 端点

| 端点 | 功能 |
|------|------|
| `POST /detectors/trace/analyze` | 完整调用链分析 |
| `POST /detectors/trace/analyze-with-flash-detect` | 闪电贷专项分析 |
| `GET /detectors/trace/supported-chains` | 6条链列表 |
| `GET /detectors/trace/signatures?prefix=0xa9` | 签名前缀查询 |
| `GET /detectors/trace/stats` | 签名库统计 |

### 前端 (P4) — 7 个新文件

```
frontend/src/
├── api/trace.js                          # API 封装
├── stores/traceAnalysis.js              # Pinia Store
├── views/ChainAnalysis.vue               # 主页面
└── components/analysis/
    ├── CallTreeView.vue                  # 调用树容器 + 工具栏
    ├── CallNode.vue                      # 单节点渲染 (类型颜色/签名/Events)
    ├── SignatureTag.vue                 # 签名标签 + "?" 查询按钮
    ├── BehaviorPanel.vue                 # 行为检测结果卡片
    └── TokenFlowPanel.vue               # Token 流转 IN/OUT 摘要
```

全部开发完成。以下是完整的交付总结：

---

## 开发交付总结

### 后端 (P0 + P1) — 13 个新文件

```
detectors/
├── __init__.py                          # 模块入口
└── trace/                               # ★ 新子模块
    ├── __init__.py                      # 统一导出
    ├── models.py (8.1KB)                # 数据模型: CallNode, FullAnalysisResult 等
    ├── provider.py (10.4KB)             # MultiRpcClient: 6链 x 多节点自动切换
    ├── signature_db.py (9.3KB)           # SignatureDB: SQLite + 内置 fallback 20条
    ├── protocol_registry.py (6.9KB)     # ProtocolRegistry: ETH 10 + BSC 11 协议
    ├── analyzer.py (25.3KB)             # ★ TxTraceAnalyzer 基类: RPC→树→签名→协议→Event
    └── behaviors/                       # 行为检测器 (继承基类)
        ├── __init__.py
        ├── base_behavior.py (5.1KB)     # BaseBehaviorDetector: 通用工具方法
        ├── flash_loan.py (8.0KB)         # FlashLoanDetector: 3种检测策略
        └── token_flow.py (5.8KB)         # TokenFlowDetector: 大额转账+swap序列

routers/detectors/
├── __init__.py
└── trace_router.py                       # 5个API端点

scripts/
├── verify_trace_module.py               # P0/P1 集成验证脚本
└── download_4bytes/sync_4bytes.py       # 改造版: 写入SQLite, 支持增量
```

### API 端点

| 端点 | 功能 |
|------|------|
| `POST /detectors/trace/analyze` | 完整调用链分析 |
| `POST /detectors/trace/analyze-with-flash-detect` | 闪电贷专项分析 |
| `GET /detectors/trace/supported-chains` | 6条链列表 |
| `GET /detectors/trace/signatures?prefix=0xa9` | 签名前缀查询 |
| `GET /detectors/trace/stats` | 签名库统计 |

### 前端 (P4) — 7 个新文件

```
frontend/src/
├── api/trace.js                          # API 封装
├── stores/traceAnalysis.js              # Pinia Store
├── views/ChainAnalysis.vue               # 主页面
└── components/analysis/
    ├── CallTreeView.vue                  # 调用树容器 + 工具栏
    ├── CallNode.vue                      # 单节点渲染 (类型颜色/签名/Events)
    ├── SignatureTag.vue                 # 签名标签 + "?" 查询按钮
    ├── BehaviorPanel.vue                 # 行为检测结果卡片
    └── TokenFlowPanel.vue               # Token 流转 IN/OUT 摘要
```

### 验证结果

```
[PASS] 全部 13 个后端模块导入正常
[PASS] 数据模型 JSON 序列化 (152K chars)
[PASS] 签名库 lookup/prefix_search/bulk_query
[PASS] RPC 分析: 151 节点 / 深度11 / 56 Events / 35 Selectors / 2.5s
[PASS] Aave V3 Pool 协议识别
[PASS] FlashLoanDetector 继承 + 检测运行正常
[PASS] 前端零 lint 错误
```

## 改动总结 — BlockSec 风格升级

### CallNode.vue（核心改动）
| 之前 | 现在 (BlockSec 风格) |
|------|---------------------|
| 树形缩进线 (`\|--`) | **深度数字指示器** `0→` `1→` `2→` |
| 类型在左侧 | **类型 + Gas + 地址** 水平排列 |
| 签名和参数混在一起 | **签名(黄色) + 参数(灰色)** 分离，参数智能格式化（address缩短、大数可读化） |
| Value 显示 `ETH/Gwei` | **Value 用逗号分隔** `, 100,000,000 ETH` |
| 输出值显示 | **箭头 → (返回值)** 绿色样式 |
| Event 单行 | **EVENT [Contract] Name, detail** 蓝绿配色 |

### CallTreeView.vue
- 工具栏：**Expand/Default/StaticCall/GasUsed/SStore/SLoad** 开关
- 搜索框：**Search by contract label / address**
- Debug 按钮
- GitHub Dark 风格配色

### ChainAnalysis.vue / BehaviorPanel / TokenFlowPanel / SignatureTag
- 全部统一为 **GitHub Dark** 配色方案 (`#0d1117` / `#161b22` / `#21262d`)
- 字体使用 **JetBrains Mono** 等专业等宽字体

## 改动总结 — BlockSec 风格升级

### CallNode.vue（核心改动）
| 之前 | 现在 (BlockSec 风格) |
|------|---------------------|
| 树形缩进线 (`\|--`) | **深度数字指示器** `0→` `1→` `2→` |
| 类型在左侧 | **类型 + Gas + 地址** 水平排列 |
| 签名和参数混在一起 | **签名(黄色) + 参数(灰色)** 分离，参数智能格式化（address缩短、大数可读化） |
| Value 显示 `ETH/Gwei` | **Value 用逗号分隔** `, 100,000,000 ETH` |
| 输出值显示 | **箭头 → (返回值)** 绿色样式 |
| Event 单行 | **EVENT [Contract] Name, detail** 蓝绿配色 |

### CallTreeView.vue
- 工具栏：**Expand/Default/StaticCall/GasUsed/SStore/SLoad** 开关
- 搜索框：**Search by contract label / address**
- Debug 按钮
- GitHub Dark 风格配色

### ChainAnalysis.vue / BehaviorPanel / TokenFlowPanel / SignatureTag
- 全部统一为 **GitHub Dark** 配色方案 (`#0d1117` / `#161b22` / `#21262d`)
- 字体使用 **JetBrains Mono** 等专业等宽字体

{"type":"reasoning","text":"找到问题了！**SignatureTag.vue 第 30 行：`const sig = computed(() => props.functionSig)` — 这里使用了 `props` 但 `props` 没有被定义！**\n\n在 `<script setup>` 中，`defineProps` 返回的 props 对象需要显式接收。当前代码没有将 `defineProps()` 的返回值赋给 `props` 变量，所以 `props.functionSig` 会报 **ReferenceError: props is not defined**。\n\n这就是为什么整个 CallNode 组件树无法渲染的原因 — SignatureTag 在渲染时崩溃了。\n\n修复方法：\n```javascript\nconst props = defineProps({\n  selector: { type: String, default: '' },\n  functionSig: { type: String, default: '' },\n})\n```\n\n让我修复这个问题。"}

**找到根因了！** `SignatureTag.vue:30` 使用了 `props.functionSig`，但 **没有用 `const props = defineProps(...)` 接收返回值**，导致 `props is not defined` 运行时错误，整个组件树崩溃不渲染。

<user_query>
shm和wal后缀的签名数据库是啥?
</user_query>




---

**CodeBuddy**

{"type":"reasoning","text":"The user is asking about the `.shm` and `.wal` files they see alongside their SQLite database. This is because we set `PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL` in the script."}

这是 SQLite **WAL 模式**（Write-Ahead Logging）自动生成的文件，正常现象。

| 文件 | 作用 |
|------|------|
| `signatures.db` | 主数据库文件（实际数据） |
| `signatures.db-wal` | WAL 日志（写入先写这里，再合并回主文件） |
| `signatures.db-shm` | 共享内存索引（加速 WAL 的并发读取） |

**为什么有它们？** 脚本里设置了：
```python
conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL")   # 第457行
```

**WAL vs 默认 DELETE 模式的区别：**

| | WAL 模式 | 默认 DELETE 模式 |
|--|---------|-----------------|
| 并发读写 | 读不阻塞写，写不阻塞读 | 写时锁整个 DB |
| 性能 | 高并发写入更快 | 简单场景够用 |
| 额外文件 | `.wal` + `.shm` | 无 |

**可以安全删除吗？** 不建议直接删。正确做法是在关闭连接前 checkpoint：

```bash
# 同步完成后，把 WAL 合并回主文件
sqlite3 data/signatures.db "PRAGMA wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE);"
```

或者脚本结束时加一步。要我给脚本加上同步完成后的自动 checkpoint 吗？这样 `.wal` 和 `.shm` 就会自动清理。

代码完整。总结改动：

## `signature_db.py` 三级查询链路

```
lookup("0xa9059cbb")
  │
  ├─ Level 1: 内置 _BUILTIN_SIGNATURES (内存，O(1))     ← 命中即返回
  │
  ├─ Level 2: 本地 SQLite (跳过 Unknown 条目)           ← 命中即返回
  │
  ├─ Level 3: 4byte.directory API                       ← 命中则写入 DB 并返回
  │     GET /api/v1/signatures/?hex_signature=0xa9059cbb
  │
  └─ Level 4: 标记 "Unknown(0xa9059cbb)" → 写入 DB      ← 避免重复查询
```

**关键改动：**

| 方法 | 改动 |
|------|------|
| `lookup()` | 从纯 DB 查询改为 **DB → API → Unknown** 链式查询 |
| `bulk_lookup()` | 新增 **并发 API 回退**（ThreadPoolExecutor，最多 8 线程） |
| `_query_4byte_api()` | 单个 selector API 查询 |
| `_bulk_query_4byte_api()` | 批量并发 API（分析交易时一次查几十个 selector） |
| `_save_to_db()` | API 结果自动入库 + 频率累加 |
| `get_stats()` | 新增 `unknown_count` 和 `api_enabled` 字段 |

**API 接口使用：**
```
GET https://www.4byte.directory/api/v1/signatures/?hex_signature=0xSELECTOR
→ {"count": N, "results": [{"id":1, "text_signature":"transfer(address,uint256)", "hex_signature":"0xa9059cbb", ...}]}
```

**前端点击 `?` 按钮时** 的流程也走同一套三级链路（通过 `/detectors/trace/signatures` 路由调用 `SignatureDB.lookup()`）。

Arguments: {"filePath":"d:\\Programming\\Python\\AlertProcessor\\scripts\\sync_signatures.py","old_str":"def save_batch(conn: sqlite3.Connection, batch: list[tuple]) -> int:\n    if not batch:\n        return 0\n    try:\n        cur = conn.cursor()\n        cur.executemany(\n            \"INSERT OR IGNORE INTO signatures (selector, text_sig, num_results) \"\n            \"VALUES (?, ?, ?)\",\n            batch,\n        )\n        conn.commit()\n        return cur.rowcount\n    except Exception as e:\n        log.error(\"批量写入失败: %s\", e)\n        conn.rollback()\n        return 0","new_str":"def save_batch(conn: sqlite3.Connection, batch: list[tuple]) -> int:\n    if not batch:\n        return 0\n    try:\n        cur = conn.cursor()\n        # 用前后计数差值计算实际写入数（INSERT OR IGNORE 的 rowcount 不可靠）\n        before = conn.execute(\"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM signatures\").fetchone()[0]\n        cur.executemany(\n            \"INSERT OR IGNORE INTO signatures (selector, text_sig, num_results) \"\n            \"VALUES (?, ?, ?)\",\n            batch,\n        )\n        conn.commit()\n        after = conn.execute(\"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM signatures\").fetchone()[0]\n        return after - before\n    except Exception as e:\n        log.error(\"批量写入失败: %s\", e)\n        conn.rollback()\n        return 0"}
<result><omitted /></result>

<previous_assistant_message>
**根因找到了：`INSERT OR IGNORE` 的 `cursor.rowcount` 在 SQLite 某些版本下始终返回 0**（即使数据实际写入了）。所以 `inserted=0` 不代表没写入，只是统计方式有误。

修复方案：改用 **写入前后的 `COUNT(*)` 差值** 来计算实际新增行数。

重新运行，现在日志应该会正确显示：
```
[Page ~ 10] 获取=1,000  插入=1,000  跳过=0  待写=0
[Page ~ 20] 获取=2,000  插入=2,000  跳过=0  待写=0
```
</previous_assistant_message>

</user_query>

<previous_assistant_message>
当前脚本**不做冲突检查**。具体来说：

**现状：**
- `UNIQUE(selector, text_signature)` — 只保证同一对 `(selector, 签名文本)` 不重复
- 同一个 `selector` 可以存多个不同的 `text_signature`（4byte 本身就是这样，一个 `0xa9059cbb` 可能对应 `transfer(address,uint256)` 和其他变体）
- 查询时 `SignatureDB.lookup()` 取第一条

**潜在问题：** 如果 4byte 返回了垃圾/错误签名，也会原样入库。

你期望哪种冲突策略？

1. **保持现状** — 多签名共存，查询时取第一个（简单）
2. **按频率优先** — 同一 selector 只保留 `num_results` 最高的那条（更干净）
3. **严格去重** — 每个 selector 仅保留一条，后续签名全部忽略

同一 selector 下，保留出现次数最多（num_results 最大）的签名\n      - 删除其余低置信度签名\n      - 可先 --cleanup-dry-run 预览将要删除的数量\n    \n    Returns: 清洗统计\n    \"\"\"\n    log.info(\"开始冲突清洗...\")\n    \n    total_rows = conn.execute(\"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM signatures\").fetchone()[0]\n    unique_sel = conn.execute(\"SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT selector) FROM signatures\").fetchone()[0]\n    \n    # 找出每个 selector 的最佳行 id（num_results 最大，若相等则取 id 最小的）\n    best_ids = set()\n    for row in conn.execute(\"\"\"\n        SELECT MAX(id) as keep_id FROM (\n            SELECT id, selector, num_results,\n                   ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY selector ORDER BY num_results DESC, id ASC) as rn\n            FROM signatures\n        )\n        WHERE rn = 1 GROUP BY selector\n    \"\"\").fetchall():\n        best_ids.add(row[0])\n    \n    # 也可以用更简单的子查询（兼容性更好）:\n    if not best_ids:\n        # 回退方案：用子查询找每组的 max(id)\n        for row in conn.execute(\"\"\"\n            SELECT MAX(id) FROM signatures GROUP BY selector\n        \"\"\").fetchall():\n            best_ids.add(row[0])\n    \n    conflicts_to_remove = total_rows - <previous_assistant_message>
完成。总结改动：

### 冲突处理机制（3 层）

**1. 写入时 — `save_batch()` 已改为智能 upsert**
```
同一 (selector, text_sig) 重复出现 → num_results +1（记录频率）
全新签名 → 直接 INSERT
```
不再盲目 `INSERT OR IGNORE`，而是**累加频率计数**。

**2. 清洗命令 — `--cleanup` / `--cleanup-dry-run`**
```bash
# 先预览效果（不删）
python scripts/sync_signatures.py --cleanup-dry-run

# 确认后执行清洗：每个 selector 只保留 num_results 最高的那条
python scripts/sync_signatures.py --cleanup
```

**3. 查询时 — `SignatureDB.lookup()` 取最佳匹配**
查询时取每个 selector 的第一条（即 `num_results` 最高的），无需改动。

### 使用建议
```bash
# 先全量拉入（允许重复）
python scripts/sync_signatures.py --workers 10

# 拉完后清洗一次，每个 selector 只留最佳签名
python scripts/sync_signatures.py --cleanup

# 之后增量更新 + 清洗
python scripts/sync_signatures.py --incremental --cleanup
```
</previous_assistant_message>

<previous_user_message><user_query>
shm和wal后缀的签名数据库是啥?
</user_query>
</previous_user_message>

<previous_assistant_message>
这是 SQLite **WAL 模式**（Write-Ahead Logging）自动生成的文件，正常现象。

| 文件 | 作用 |
|------|------|
| `signatures.db` | 主数据库文件（实际数据） |
| `signatures.db-wal` | WAL 日志（写入先写这里，再合并回主文件） |
| `signatures.db-shm` | 共享内存索引（加速 WAL 的并发读取） |

**为什么有它们？** 脚本里设置了：
```python
conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL")   # 第457行
```

**WAL vs 默认 DELETE 模式的区别：**

| | WAL 模式 | 默认 DELETE 模式 |
|--|---------|-----------------|
| 并发读写 | 读不阻塞写，写不阻塞读 | 写时锁整个 DB |
| 性能 | 高并发写入更快 | 简单场景够用 |
| 额外文件 | `.wal` + `.shm` | 无 |

**可以安全删除吗？** 不建议直接删。正确做法是在关闭连接前 checkpoint：

```bash
# 同步完成后，把 WAL 合并回主文件
sqlite3 data/signatures.db "PRAGMA wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE);"
```

或者脚本结束时加一步。要我给脚本加上同步完成后的自动 checkpoint 吗？这样 `.wal` 和 `.shm` 就会自动清理。
</previous_assistant_message>

<previous_assistant_message>
代码完整。总结改动：

## `signature_db.py` 三级查询链路

```
lookup("0xa9059cbb")
  │
  ├─ Level 1: 内置 _BUILTIN_SIGNATURES (内存，O(1))     ← 命中即返回
  │
  ├─ Level 2: 本地 SQLite (跳过 Unknown 条目)           ← 命中即返回
  │
  ├─ Level 3: 4byte.directory API                       ← 命中则写入 DB 并返回
  │     GET /api/v1/signatures/?hex_signature=0xa9059cbb
  │
  └─ Level 4: 标记 "Unknown(0xa9059cbb)" → 写入 DB      ← 避免重复查询
```

**关键改动：**

| 方法 | 改动 |
|------|------|
| `lookup()` | 从纯 DB 查询改为 **DB → API → Unknown** 链式查询 |
| `bulk_lookup()` | 新增 **并发 API 回退**（ThreadPoolExecutor，最多 8 线程） |
| `_query_4byte_api()` | 单个 selector API 查询 |
| `_bulk_query_4byte_api()` | 批量并发 API（分析交易时一次查几十个 selector） |
| `_save_to_db()` | API 结果自动入库 + 频率累加 |
| `get_stats()` | 新增 `unknown_count` 和 `api_enabled` 字段 |

**API 接口使用：**
```
GET https://www.4byte.directory/api/v1/signatures/?hex_signature=0xSELECTOR
→ {"count": N, "results": [{"id":1, "text_signature":"transfer(address,uint256)", "hex_signature":"0xa9059cbb", ...}]}
```

**前端点击 `?` 按钮时** 的流程也走同一套三级链路（通过 `/detectors/trace/signatures` 路由调用 `SignatureDB.lookup()`）。
</previous_assistant_message>

## 修改内容

### 后端 `signature_db.py` — 核心改动

| 方法 | 改动 |
|------|------|
| 新增 **`lookup_all(selector)`** | 返回 `list[str]` 全部候选签名（三级查询） |
| **`lookup()`** | 兼容旧接口，内部调用 `lookup_all()[0]` 取最佳匹配 |
| 新增 **`_query_4byte_api_all()`** | API 查询返回全部 `results[]` 而非只取第一个 |
| 新增 **`lookup_by_hex()`** | 供路由使用，返回结构化 `{selector, signatures:[{text,num_results}], total, source}` |
| **`_lookup_db_all()`** | DB 查询返回该 selector 的全部签名列表 |
| **`bulk_lookup()`** | 返回值从 `{sel: str}` → `{sel: list[str]}` |
| `_save_to_db` | 修复列名错误 `text_sig` → `text_signature` |

### 路由 `trace_router.py`

- `/signatures?hex=0xa9059cbb` — **新增精确查询**，返回该 selector 的全部候选签名
- `/signatures?prefix=0xa90` — 前缀搜索保持不变

### 前端

**API 层** (`trace.js`)：新增 `lookupSignatures(hex)` 精确查询函数

**Store** (`traceAnalysis.js`)：`resolveSignature` 改用 `lookupSignatures`，缓存多签名数据

**SignatureTag.vue** — 重写显示逻辑：
```
已知签名: transfer(address,uint256) +3          ← 点击 +N 展开
未知签名: [0xabc...] [?] → transfer(...) +2     ← 点击 ? 查询
                                  ├─ transfer(address,uint256)   ← 主签名(绿色)
                                  ├─ transfer(address,address)    ← 候选(灰色)
                                  └─ transferFrom(...)           ← 候选(灰色)
```

## `CallNode.vue` 重写（仿 BlockSec 风格）

**主行（紧凑单行）：**
```
▼ 0  CALL  Uniswap V2 Router 02  , swapExactTokensForTokens(...) value= 0.1234 ETH (...) [☰]
▲ 1  CALL  0xf32812...133b       (to=Forwarder, amount=13,900,000,000, ...)          [☰]
   1  STATIC 569  0xf32812...133b  (value= 92,008,480,308,556,800)                    [☰]
```

**关键优化：**

| 改动 | 说明 |
|------|------|
| **参数截断** | 只显示前4个参数，总长度限制70字符，超长用 `\u2026` |
| **地址截断** | To 地址 max-width: 140px，溢出省略 |
| **Output 截断** | 输出数据 max-width: 120px，hover 显示完整内容 |
| **Error 截断** | Revert 消息 max-width: 200px |
| **Raw data 按钮** `[☰]` | 右侧按钮（hover 才显示），点击展开详情面板 |

<analysis>
The conversation covers 5 major phases of work on an Ethereum transaction analysis tool (AlertProcessor):

1. **Multi-signature support fix**: User pointed out that one function selector (4-byte hex) can map to multiple function signatures. The original code only returned the best match. I rewrote `signature_db.py` to return all candidate signatures, updated the router, API layer, store, and SignatureTag.vue component.

2. **IndentationError fix**: After the rewrite, there was a duplicate `for r in rows:` line causing IndentationError at line 494. Fixed by removing the duplicate line.

3. **Runtime error fix (list has no attribute 'find')**: After changing `bulk_lookup()` return type from `{sel: str}` to `{sel: list[str]}`, `analyzer.py` was still treating the result as a string, causing `.find()` to fail. Fixed by extracting the first (best) signature from the list in analyzer.py.

4. **CallNode.vue UI optimization (BlockSec-style)**: User showed screenshot of left-side data being too long. Rewrote CallNode.vue with compact single-line display, parameter truncation, address truncation, and expandable detail panel on click.

5. **TokenFlowPanel + BehaviorPanel beautification**: User showed screenshot of Token Flows being unclear with UNKNOWN tokens everywhere. Completely rewrote both TokenFlowPanel.vue (grouped by token with summary bar) and BehaviorPanel.vue (risk-level colored borders, icons, confidence bars).

Key files modified:
- `detectors/trace/signature_db.py` - Core signature DB service
- `routers/detectors/trace_router.py` - API routes
- `detectors/trace/analyzer.py` - Transaction trace analyzer
- `frontend/src/api/trace.js` - Frontend API layer
- `frontend/src/stores/traceAnalysis.js` - Pinia store
- `frontend/src/components/analysis/SignatureTag.vue` - Signature tag component
- `frontend/src/components/analysis/CallNode.vue` - Call tree node (rewritten)
- `frontend/src/components/analysis/TokenFlowPanel.vue` - Token flow panel (rewritten)
- `frontend/src/components/analysis/BehaviorPanel.vue` - Behavior/risk panel (rewritten)
- `frontend/src/views/ChainAnalysis.vue` - Main view page (read for context)
</analysis>

<summary>
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   - **Fix multi-signature logic**: User identified that a single function selector can correspond to multiple function names in the database. The code should show ALL matching signatures, not just the best one.
   - **Fix runtime errors**: Resolve IndentationError and `'list' object has no attribute 'find'` errors introduced during the refactor.
   - **Optimize CallNode UI (BlockSec-style)**: Left-side call tree data was too long/incomplete. User wanted compact single-line display like BlockSec's interface, with click-to-expand detail panels.
   - **Beautify TokenFlows + Behavior panels**: Right-side Token Flows panel was unclear (lots of UNKNOWN labels). User wanted clearer, more intuitive display.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - Ethereum 4-byte function selectors mapping to multiple text signatures (multi-ambiguity)
   - Three-level cache lookup: Built-in → Local SQLite DB → 4byte.directory API
   - Vue 3 Composition API + Pinia state management
   - FastAPI backend with Python
   - BlockSec-inspired UI pattern: compact tree nodes + expandable detail panels

3. Files and Code Sections:

   - **`detectors/trace/signature_db.py`** — Core signature database service. Major refactoring:
     - Added `lookup_all(selector)` → returns `list[str]` of all candidates
     - Added `_query_4byte_api_all(selector)` → returns all results from 4byte API
     - Added `lookup_by_hex(hex_sig)` → structured response for API route: `{selector, signatures:[{text,num_results}], total, source}`
     - Changed `bulk_lookup()` return type: `{sel: str}` → `{sel: list[str]}`
     - Added `_lookup_db_all(selector)` → returns all DB entries per selector
     - Fixed column name bug: `text_sig` → `text_signature` in `_save_to_db()`
     - Fixed duplicate `for r in rows:` line (IndentationError)

   - **`detectors/trace/analyzer.py`** — Transaction trace analyzer:
     - Fixed line ~307-312 to handle new list-type return from `bulk_lookup()`:
     ```python
     # Before: sig = sig_map.get(sel)  # returned str
     # After:
     sig_list = sig_map.get(sel)
     sig = sig_list[0] if isinstance(sig_list, list) and len(sig_list) > 0 else sig_list
     ```

   - **`routers/detectors/trace_router.py`** — Added `?hex=` param to `/signatures` endpoint for exact multi-signature query alongside existing `?prefix=` search.

   - **`frontend/src/api/trace.js`** — Added `lookupSignatures(hex)` function calling `?hex=` endpoint.

   - **`frontend/src/stores/traceAnalysis.js`** — Updated `resolveSignature()` to use `lookupSignatures()` instead of prefix search; now caches full multi-signature data structure.

   - **`frontend/src/components/analysis/SignatureTag.vue`** — Fully rewritten to show primary signature + `+N` count badge, click to expand all candidates with color coding (green=primary, gray=candidates).

   - **`frontend/src/components/analysis/CallNode.vue`** — Fully rewritten to BlockSec-style compact layout:
     - Single-line: depth indicator ▼/▲ | index | CALL type | contract name/address | truncated params | value | status icon | [☰] raw data button
     - Parameter truncation: max 4 params, 70 char limit
     - Address truncation with max-width CSS
     - Click row or ☰ button to expand detail panel showing full from/to/function/data/value/gas/output
     - Color-coded by CALL type (green=call, blue=delegatecall, purple=staticcall, red=revert/error)

   - **`frontend/src/components/analysis/TokenFlowPanel.vue`** — Fully rewritten:
     - Summary bar at top: total IN / OUT / token count
     - Grouped by token symbol into cards (WETH, USDT, UNKNOWN, etc.)
     - Each transfer shows direction arrow (→ IN green / ← OUT red), address label + short address, formatted amount (K/M/B auto-formatting)
     - Empty state handling

   - **`frontend/src/components/analysis/BehaviorPanel.vue`** — Fully rewritten:
     - Left border color-coded by risk level (red/yellow/green)
     - Risk icons: ☠ high risk / ⚠ warning / ✓ safe
     - Confidence gradient progress bar
     - Detail JSON with long-value truncation
     - Clean empty state

4. Errors and fixes:
   - **IndentationError at line 494** (`signature_db.py`): Duplicate `for r in rows:` statement after a code replacement. Fixed by removing the duplicate line.
   - **`'list' object has no attribute 'find'`**: After changing `bulk_lookup()` to return `{sel: list[str]}`, `analyzer.py` line ~328 called `.find()` on what was now a list. Fixed by extracting first element: `sig = sig_list[0] if isinstance(sig_list, list) and len(sig_list) > 0 else sig_list`.

5. Problem Solving:
   - Solved multi-signature ambiguity across the full stack (DB → API → Store → Component)
   - Solved UI information density problem with truncation + expandable details pattern
   - Solved token flow readability with grouping and visual hierarchy

6. All user messages:
   - "之前搞错了，数据库中一个函数签名对应的函数名称可能是多个，帮我修正之前的代码错误逻辑，我希望能够显示出全部的函数签名"

   - "优化前端界面，目前左侧data数据过长，显示不完全，模仿blocksec页面，点击后显示具体信息。" (with two screenshots: current state and BlockSec reference)
   - "右侧面板Token FLows不太清晰，看不明白，美化前端页面" (with screenshot of unclear Token Flows panel)

### 修复内容

**1. 后端 — ERC20 数据缺失根因修复** (`detectors/trace/analyzer.py`)

**问题根因**: `compute_balance_changes()` 只从 `node.events` 提取 ERC20 转账，而 `node.events` 的数据来自 `link_events()` 的启发式匹配算法——该算法可能将 Transfer 事件错误关联或完全丢失。

**修复方案** — 新增 `_extract_erc20_balances(receipt_logs)` 静态方法：
- **直接从原始 receipt logs 解码 Transfer 事件**（topic0 匹配 + topics[1]/[2] 取 from/to + data 取 value）
- 绕过不可靠的事件-节点关联逻辑
- 原有 `node.events` 提取作为兜底补充

**未注册代币显示优化**：
- 注册表有名称 → 显示名称（如 WETH）
- 未注册 → 显示截断地址大写（如 `0X80F0C1...`），而非 "UNKNOWN"

**2. 前端 — 悬浮 + 复制功能** (`BalanceChangesPanel.vue`)

| 元素 | 悬浮行为 | 点击行为 |
|------|---------|---------|
| **地址标签** | 显示完整文本 (如 `WETH9 (0x80f...)`) | 复制完整地址 |
| **地址 Hash** | 显示完整 42 字符地址 | 复制地址 |
| **TokenID 列** | 显示完整 Token 合约地址 | 复制地址 |
| TokenID 行尾 | 出现复制图标 `&#8464;` | - |

<summary>
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   - **Bug fix #1**: Fix left panel's detail section not being collapsible after expanding
   - **Feature: Balance Changes module** + **Layout redesign**: Convert from left-right split panels to vertical full-width scrollable layout (mimicking BlockSec Phalcon Explorer), add a new Balance Changes panel showing per-address token/ETH balance deltas

2. Key Technical Concepts:

修复完成。两个 bug 的根因：

1. **`CallTreeView.vue:85`** — `store.root.to` 应为 `store.root.toAddress`（后端返回的字段名是 `toAddress`，不是 `to`）。这导致 `rootKey` computed 属性返回 `0-[]-undefined`，进而导致 `selectedNodeId` 比对失败和节点 key 不一致。

2. **`BalanceChangesPanel.vue:173`** — 调用了不存在的 `shorten_addr()` 函数，实际定义的函数名为 `short_addr()`。这个 `ReferenceError` 会导致整个组件渲染崩溃。

这两个 JS 运行时错误会阻断 Vue 组件树的渲染链，使页面永远停在 "Tracing transaction..." 状态。刷新页面后应该能正常显示分析结果了。
</previous_assistant_message>

修复完成，0 个 lint 错误。

**问题原因**：地址列和 Total 列使用 `v-if="ri === 0"` 条件渲染，非首行时 DOM 元素完全移除。flex 布局下每行子元素数量不一致（首行6个，后续行4-5个），导致所有列向左错位。

**修复方案**：两列始终渲染占位：
- **地址列**：非首行用 `visibility: hidden` 隐藏内容但保留空间
- **Total 列**：非首行渲染空字符串

这样每行始终保持 6 列等宽对齐。刷新页面即可看到整齐的表格。

---

## 重构总结

### 架构变更对比

**Before（单体模式）**：
```
用户点击 Analyze
  → 1 个 POST /analyze 请求 (等待全部计算完成)
    → 返回完整 ~90KB JSON
      → 存入 store.result
        → 各面板从 result 中读取切片数据
```
**After（拆分并发模式）**：
```
用户点击 Analyalyze
  → Promise.allSettled 并发发起 4 个独立请求:
    ├─ POST /call-tree       → CallTreeView (先返回先渲染)
    ├─ POST /balance-changes → BalanceChangesPanel
    ├─ POST /token-flows     → TokenFlowPanel
    └─ POST /behaviors       → BehaviorPanel
  → 各面板独立 loading/error 状态，互不阻塞
```

### 修改的文件清单

| 层 | 文件 | 变更 |
|---|------|------|
| **后端路由** | `routers/detectors/trace_router.py` | +4 新端点: `/call-tree`, `/balance-changes`, `/token-flows`, `/behaviors` |
| **前端 API** | `frontend/src/api/trace.js` | +4 新函数: `fetchCallTree`, `fetchBalanceChanges`, `fetchTokenFlows`, `fetchBehaviors` |
| **状态管理** | `frontend/src/stores/traceAnalysis.js` | 新增面板独立 state/getters/actions, 核心 action: `loadPanelsConcurrent()` |
| **页面** | `frontend/src/views/ChainAnalysis.vue` | 切换为 `loadPanelsConcurrent()`, 移除 props 传递 |
| **CallTreeView** | `.../CallTreeView.vue` | 使用 `store.isCallTreeLoading` + 错误展示 |
| **BalanceChangesPanel** | `.../BalanceChangesPanel.vue` | 支持独立加载模式 + loading/error UI |
| **TokenFlowPanel** | `.../TokenFlowPanel.vue` | 支持独立加载模式 + loading/error UI |
| **BehaviorPanel** | `.../BehaviorPanel.vue` | 使用 `store.isBehaviorLoading` + 错误展示 |

### 关键设计决策

1. **向后兼容**: 原有 `/analyze` 端点保留不变；getters 采用"优先独立数据 -> 回退全量结果"策略，确保两种模式都能正常工作
2. **容错隔离**: 某个面板请求失败不影响其他面板渲染（`Promise.allSettled`）
3. **UI 响应性**: 各面板有独立的 loading spinner 和错误提示，用户能感知到每个面板的状态
4. **0 lint 错误**: 所有文件通过静态检查

<analysis>
1. **Initial Request**: User provided a screenshot of the FundFlow project architecture and asked to analyze the existing architecture, evaluate feasibility of splitting API call logic across three panels, refactor frontend code so each panel independently makes HTTP requests and handles its own data rendering, ensuring performance isn't affected while handling request concurrency and data synchronization.

2. **Exploration Phase**: Read multiple files to understand current architecture:
   - `stores/traceAnalysis.js` — Pinia store managing all analysis state
   - `views/ChainAnalysis.vue` — Main page orchestrating panels
   - `api/trace.js` — Frontend API layer (single `analyzeTransaction` function)
   - `api/index.js` — API service configuration
   - `routers/detectors/trace_router.py` — Backend FastAPI router with single `/analyze` endpoint
   - All 4 panel components: CallTreeView, BalanceChangesPanel, TokenFlowPanel, BehaviorPanel
   - `detectors/trace/analyzer.py` — Backend analyzer supporting `run_behavior_detect` parameter

3. **Architecture Understanding**: Current flow is monolithic: one POST `/analyze` → full ~90KB JSON → stored in `store.result` → each panel reads its slice from result. User wants split endpoints.

4. **Implementation Phase**: Made changes across 8 files:
   - **Backend**: Added 4 new split endpoints + PanelRequest model
   - **Frontend API**: Added 4 new fetch functions
   - **Store**: Added per-panel state, getters with fallback strategy, concurrent loading action
   - **Views/Components**: Updated to use independent loading states

5. **Error Phase**: User ran `npm run dev` and got Babel parser errors in BalanceChangesPanel.vue and TokenFlowPanel.vue. Two issues:
   - `for...of` loop header was accidentally deleted during string replacement
   - `for (const [a, b] of ...)` destructuring syntax not supported by project's Babel config
   - Fixed by rewriting loops using index-based iteration (`for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i++)`)
</analysis>

<summary>
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   用户要求分析 FundFlow（AlertProcessor）项目的现有架构，评估将三个面板的 API 调用逻辑拆分的可行性。具体需求：
   - 重构前端代码，使每个面板（CallTreeView、BalanceChangesPanel、TokenFlowPanel、BehaviorPanel）独立发起 HTTP 请求并负责自身数据渲染
   - 确保拆分后不影响性能
   - 处理好请求并发和数据同步问题
   - 保持用户界面的响应速度和一致性

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **Pinia Store 状态管理**: 面板独立状态 + 向后兼容的全量结果回退
   - **并发请求模式**: `Promise.allSettled` 并发发起4个独立请求
   - **后端拆分端点**: 从单体 `/analyze` 拆分为 `/call-tree`, `/balance-changes`, `/token-flows`, `/behaviors`
   - **容错隔离**: 单个面板失败不影响其他面板渲染
   - **Vue 3 Composition API + Options API 混用**: 面板组件使用 `<script setup>` 或 `setup()` 函数
   - **FastAPI 后端路由**: Pydantic 模型验证 + async 端点
   - **Babel 兼容性限制**: 项目不支持 `for (const [a, b] of ...)` 解构语法和部分现代 JS 特性

3. Files and Code Sections:

   - **`routers/detectors/trace_router.py`** (后端路由)
     - 新增 `PanelRequest` Pydantic 模型（tx_hash + chain_id）
     - 新增4个拆分端点：`POST /call-tree`, `POST /balance-changes`, `POST /token-flows`, `POST /behaviors`
     - 每个端点调用 `analyzer.analyze()` 并只返回对应子集数据
     - behaviors 端点使用 `run_behavior_detect=True`，其余使用 `False`

   - **`frontend/src/api/trace.js`** (前端API层)
     - 新增4个函数: `fetchCallTree()`, `fetchBalanceChanges()`, `fetchTokenFlows()`, `fetchBehaviors()`
     - 每个 POST 到对应的拆分端点，body 为 `{ tx_hash, chain_id }`

   - **`frontend/src/stores/traceAnalysis.js`** (Pinia Store)
     - 新增面板独立状态字段:
       ```js
       callTreeData: null, isCallTreeLoading: false, callTreeError: null,
       balanceChangesData: [], isBalanceLoading: false, balanceError: null,
       tokenFlowsData: [], isTokenFlowLoading: false, tokenFlowError: null,
       behaviorsData: [], protocolsData: [], isBehaviorLoading: false, behaviorError: null
       ```
     - Getters 采用"优先独立数据 → 回退全量结果"策略（如 `root() { return this.callTreeData?.root || this.result?.root || null }`）
     - 核心新 action: `loadPanelsConcurrent()` 使用 `Promise.allSettled` 并发加载所有面板
     - 私有方法: `_loadCallTree`, `_loadBalanceChanges`, `_loadTokenFlows`, `_loadBehaviors`, `_resetPanelStates`
     - 原有 `analyzeTransaction()` 保留不变（向后兼容）

   - **`frontend/src/views/ChainAnalysis.vue`** (主页面)
     - `handleAnalyze()` 改为调用 `store.loadPanelsConcurrent()`
     - 移除面板组件的 props 传递（`:changes="..."`, `:flows="..."`, `:isLoading="..."`）
     - 按钮/输入框/select 的 disabled 状态改为 `store.isAnyPanelLoading`

   - **`frontend/src/components/analysis/CallTreeView.vue`**
     - 加载状态从 `store.isLoading` 改为 `store.isCallTreeLoading`
     - 新增错误状态展示 `store.callTreeError`
     - 新增 `.ctv-error` CSS 样式

   - **`frontend/src/components/analysis/BalanceChangesPanel.vue`**
     - 引入 store，新增 `effectiveChanges` computed（优先 props，回退 store）
     - 新增 `isLoading` 和 `hasError` computed
     - 模板中 `props.changes` 全部替换为 `effectiveChanges.value`
     - 新增 loading spinner 和 error 展示 UI
     - `groupedData`、`totalEntries`、`downloadCSV` 均改用 `effectiveChanges`
     - 循环改为索引遍历以兼容 Babel

   - **`frontend/src/components/analysis/TokenFlowPanel.vue`**
     - 同 BalanceChangesPanel 模式：引入 store，`effectiveFlows` computed
     - 新增独立 loading/error UI 和样式
     - `groupedFlows` computed 中循环改为索引遍历

   - **`frontend/src/components/analysis/BehaviorPanel.vue`**
     - `defineProps` 改为赋值给 `props` 变量
     - 新增 `effectiveLoading` computed（`props.isLoading || store.isBehaviorLoading`）
     - 新增 `hasError` computed 和错误展示 UI
     - 完整文件被重写以确保模板正确更新

4. Errors and fixes:
   - **Babel 编译错误 (2个文件)**:
     - 错误信息: `[vue/compiler-sfc] Unexpected token, expected ","` 在 BalanceChangesPanel.vue:72 和 TokenFlowPanel.vue:35
     - 根因1: 之前的字符串替换操作意外删除了 `for...of` 循环声明行，导致代码结构断裂
     - 根因2: `for (const [addr, g] of Object.entries(groups))` 解构语法在项目 Babel 配置下不支持
     - 修复方式: 将所有 `for...of` 循环重写为索引遍历 `for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i++)`，并恢复丢失的循环头
     - 修复后 lint 检查通过（0 errors）

5. Problem Solving:
   - 成功将单体架构重构为面板独立的并发加载架构
   - 解决了向后兼容性问题（保留原 `/analyze` 端点和 `analyzeTransaction` action）
   - 解决了 Babel 语法兼容性问题
   - 所有修改文件均通过 lint 检查

6. All user messages:
   - **消息1**: "分析FundFlow项目的现有架构，评估将三个面板的API调用逻辑拆分的可行性。重构前端代码，使每个面板独立发起HTTP请求并负责自身的数据渲染。确保拆分后不影响性能，处理好请求并发和数据同步问题，保持用户界面的响应速度和一致性。"（附带截图）
   - **消息2**: 用户运行 `npm run dev` 后报告了完整的 Vite/Babel 编译错误日志，包含 BalanceChangesPanel.vue 和 TokenFlowPanel.vue 的 parser 错误堆栈，要求"解决问题"
</summary>
</conversation_history_summary>

已完成 FundFlowPanel 组件的构建。以下是实现概要：

### 新增文件
- **`FundFlowPanel.vue`** — BlockSec 风格交互式资金流转图组件

### 核心功能

| 功能 | 实现方式 |
|------|----------|
| **有向图布局** | 拓扑分层算法（BFS longest-path），地址按 Sender/Receiver 角色自动排列为多列 |
| **Bezier 曲线连接** | 三次贝塞尔曲线 `C cx1,cy1 cx2,cy2 x,y`，带箭头标记和颜色渐变 |
| **缩放** | 鼠标滚轮 + 工具栏 +/- 按钮（范围 15%~400%） |
| **平移拖拽** | 鼠标左键拖拽画布（grab/grabbing 光标） |
| **自适应 Fit** | "Fit All" 按钮自动计算最佳缩放比填满视口 |
| **导出 PNG** | SVG → Canvas 2x 高清渲染 → PNG 下载（带白色背景） |
| **导出 SVG** | 直接序列化 SVG DOM 为 `.svg` 文件下载 |
| **节点高亮** | hover 节点/边时联动高亮关联元素 |
| **Sender/Receiver 标签** | 自动识别角色并显示蓝色/紫色徽章 |
| **多 Token 配色** | 10 色调色板，每 token 独立颜色 + 图例 |

### 数据兼容性

组件同时支持两种数据源：
1. **BlockSec 格式**: `{ transfers: [{from, to, amount, token, ...}] }` — 通过 `fundFlowData` prop 传入
2. **遗留格式**: store 中 `tokenFlowsData`（TokenFlowItem 数组）— 内部自动转换为 transfer 格式

### 文件修改清单

| 文件 | 改动 |
|------|------|
| `FundFlowPanel.vue` | **新建** — 完整组件 (~560 行) |
| `ChainAnalysis.vue:57-59` | 插入 `<FundFlowPanel>` 在 TokenFlows 之前 |
| `traceAnalysis.js` | 新增 `fundFlowData` state + getter + `_buildFundFlowFromTokenFlows()` 方法 |

Arguments: {"filePath":"d:\\Programming\\Python\\AlertProcessor\\frontend\\src\\stores\\traceAnalysis.js","old_str":"    async _loadTokenFlows(hash, cid) {\n      this.isTokenFlowLoading = true\n      this.tokenFlowError = null\n      try {\n        const data = await fetchTokenFlows(hash, cid)\n        this.tokenFlowsData = data.tokenFlows || []\n        console.log(`[TraceStore] Token flows loaded: ${this.tokenFlowsData.length} flows`)\n      } catch (e) {\n        this.tokenFlowError = e.message || 'Token flows failed'\n        console.warn('[TraceStore] Token flows error:', e)\n      } finally {\n        this.isTokenFlowLoading = false\n      }\n    },","new_str":"    async _loadTokenFlows(hash, cid) {\n      this.isTokenFlowLoading = true\n      this.tokenFlowError = null\n      try {\n        const data = await fetchTokenFlows(hash, cid)\n        this.tokenFlowsData = data.tokenFlows || []\n        console.log(`[TraceStore] Token flows loaded: ${this.tokenFlowsData.length} flows`)\n        // Convert to BlockSec-style fund flow format for diagram\n        this._buildFundFlowFromTokenFlows(this.tokenFlowsData)\n      } catch (e) {\n        this.tokenFlowError = e.message || 'Token flows failed'\n        console.warn('[TraceStore] Token flows error:', e)\n      } finally {\n        this.isTokenFlowLoading = false\n      }\n    },\n\n    _buildFundFlowFromTokenFlows(flows) {\n      var transfers = []\n      for (var i = 0; i < flows.length; i++) {\n        var f = flows[i]\n        transfers.push({\n          id: i + 1,\n          amount: String(f.amountRaw || 0),\n          from: f.from || '',\n          to: f.to || f.toAddress || '',\n          token: f.tokenAddress || f.token_address || '',\n          tokenType: (f.tokenAddress || f.token_address) ? 1 : 0,\n          isReverted: false,\n        })\n      }\n      this.fundFlowData = transfers.length > 0 ? { transfers: transfers } : null\n    },"}

<previous_assistant_message>
已完成 FundFlowPanel 组件的构建。以下是实现概要：

### 新增文件
- **`FundFlowPanel.vue`** — BlockSec 风格交互式资金流转图组件

### 核心功能

| 功能 | 实现方式 |
|------|----------|
| **有向图布局** | 拓扑分层算法（BFS longest-path），地址按 Sender/Receiver 角色自动排列为多列 |
| **Bezier 曲线连接** | 三次贝塞尔曲线 `C cx1,cy1 cx2,cy2 x,y`，带箭头标记和颜色渐变 |
| **缩放** | 鼠标滚轮 + 工具栏 +/- 按钮（范围 15%~400%） |
| **平移拖拽** | 鼠标左键拖拽画布（grab/grabbing 光标） |
| **自适应 Fit** | "Fit All" 按钮自动计算最佳缩放比填满视口 |
| **导出 PNG** | SVG → Canvas 2x 高清渲染 → PNG 下载（带白色背景） |
| **导出 SVG** | 直接序列化 SVG DOM 为 `.svg` 文件下载 |
| **节点高亮** | hover 节点/边时联动高亮关联元素 |
| **Sender/Receiver 标签** | 自动识别角色并显示蓝色/紫色徽章 |
| **多 Token 配色** | 10 色调色板，每 token 独立颜色 + 图例 |

### 数据兼容性

组件同时支持两种数据源：
1. **BlockSec 格式**: `{ transfers: [{from, to, amount, token, ...}] }` — 通过 `fundFlowData` prop 传入
2. **遗留格式**: store 中 `tokenFlowsData`（TokenFlowItem 数组）— 内部自动转换为 transfer 格式

### 文件修改清单

| 文件 | 改动 |
|------|------|
| `FundFlowPanel.vue` | **新建** — 完整组件 (~560 行) |
| `ChainAnalysis.vue:57-59` | 插入 `<FundFlowPanel>` 在 TokenFlows 之前 |
| `traceAnalysis.js` | 新增 `fundFlowData` state + getter + `_buildFundFlowFromTokenFlows()` 方法 |

- **tokenFlows** 数据中**没有 `from`/`to` 地址字段**（只有 `fromLabel: "0x"`, `toLabel: "0x"`）

3. 按比例在 Sender 和 Receiver 之间建立转移边（transfer edges）

### 边路由算法：BlockSec 风格正交样条曲线

```
旧: 简单三次贝塞尔 C(cx1,cy1 cx2,cy2 dx,dy) — 曲线穿过节点区域
新: 正交样条 M(sx,sy) C(p1x,p1y midX,midY p2x,p2y) endX,endY
    ├── 水平出口段 (EXIT_LEN=80px)   ← 沿源节点右边缘水平出发
    ├── 弧形过渡段 (midY+vertOff)     ← 在中间区域弯曲绕行  
    └── 水平接近段 (APPROACH_LEN=60px)← 水平进入目标节点左边缘
```

### 关键参数变更

| 参数 | 旧值 | 新值 | 效果 |
|------|------|------|------|
| `COL_GAP` | 340 | **360** | 列更宽，长标签不拥挤 |
| `ROW_GAP` | 90 | **110** | 行距更大 |
| 画布高度 | 520px | **600px** | 容纳垂直展开的曲线 |
| 并行边间距 | 22px | **28px** | 多条边分散更清晰 |

编译无错误，所有改动完成。核心修复和新增功能如下：

---

**1. 箭头渲染修复（根本原因）**

- **`markerUnits="userSpaceOnUse"`** — 之前用 `strokeWidth` 导致 marker 被放大到巨大不可见。改为 `userSpaceOnUse` 后尺寸固定 `18x14px`，精确可控
- **marker ID 用数字索引** — 之前用 token 地址（如 `#arrow-0xeee...`）作为 ID，地址中的特殊字符可能导致 SVG 引用失败。现改用 `#arr-0`, `#arr-1`... 的纯数字索引
- **三层 path 渲染策略**：
  - 底层：半透明基础线（opacity 0.65）+ **透明箭头载体 path**（仅用于渲染 marker，`stroke="transparent"`）
  - 悬浮时叠加：高亮发光动画线 + 可见箭头

**2. BlockSec 风格交互效果**

| 效果 | 实现 |
|------|------|
| **悬浮边高亮** | 鼠标进入 edge/label → 该边加粗 + glow 发光 + 背景其他边变暗 (opacity→0.15) |
| **悬浮节点联动** | 鼠标悬停节点 → 该节点高亮 + **所有关联连线同时激活**（双向：节点→边、边→节点） |
| **动态流向动画** | `@keyframes flowDash` — stroke-dashoffset 动画产生流动粒子效果，沿路径从源向目标移动 |
| **颜色联动** | 高亮节点的边框颜色 = 关联边的 token 颜色（而非统一的蓝色），视觉上更直观 |

**3. 交互状态管理优化**
- `isEdgeActive(id)` / `isNodeActive(addr)` — 统一判断逻辑，支持 node↔edge 双向激活
- `activeFromLabel` 标志防止 label hover 和 node hover 冲突
- `onCanvasLeave` — 鼠标离开画布时清除所有 hover 状态

<summary>
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   - **Initial request**: Fix arrow visibility issues and node/text overlapping in the FundFlowPanel SVG diagram. Ensure clear visual hierarchy with no element occlusion. User suggested considering D3.js/ECharts/React Flow if current approach was insufficient.
   - **Follow-up request**: Arrows still not showing after first fix — prioritize fixing arrow rendering. Additionally implement BlockSec-style interactive effects: hover-highlighting of related nodes/edges, and dynamic flow direction animation along transfer paths.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - Vue 3 Options API with `setup()` function (Composition API patterns)
   - Pure SVG-based directed graph visualization (no external library)
   - SVG `<marker>` elements for arrowheads — critical distinction between `markerUnits="strokeWidth"` (scales with stroke, caused bugs) vs `"userSpaceOnUse"` (fixed pixel size, correct solution)
   - Topological sorting / BFS longest-path layering for node layout
   - Cubic Bézier edge routing with parallel edge offset and layer-aware curve avoidance
   - 4-layer SVG rendering order: Grid dots → Edge paths → Node cards → Edge labels
   - CSS `@keyframes` stroke-dashoffset animation for flow particle effect
   - Bidirectional hover state propagation (node ↔ edge)
   - Dual-path technique: transparent path carries marker, visible path carries animation

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - **`d:\Programming\Python\AlertProcessor\frontend\src\components\analysis\FundFlowPanel.vue`**
     - The sole file modified across all interactions. Complete rewrite performed twice.
     - **Final version key architectural decisions:**
       - Marker definition uses numeric IDs to avoid token address special characters:
         ```html
         <marker :id="'arr-'+ti" markerWidth="18" markerHeight="14" refX="16" refY="7"
                 orient="auto" markerUnits="userSpaceOnUse">
           <path d="M0,0 L18,7 L0,14 L5,7 z" :fill="tc.color"/>
         </marker>
         ```
       - Arrow fix: `ARROW_GAP = 14`, `markerUnits="userSpaceOnUse"`, `refX=16`
       - Each edge renders TWO paths — one transparent for the marker, one visible for the animation:
         ```html
         <!-- Transparent carrier for arrowhead marker -->
         <path :d="edge.d" stroke="transparent" stroke-width="2.5" fill="none"
               :marker-end="edge.mid" />
         <!-- Visible animated path -->
         <path :d="edge.d" :stroke="edge.color" stroke-width="2" fill="none"
               class="ffp-flow-line" :class="{'ffp-flow-active': isEdgeActive(edge.id)}"
               :style="{ '--flow-color': edge.color }" />
         ```
       - Flow animation via CSS:
         ```css
         @keyframes flowDash { to { stroke-dashoffset: -24; } }
         .ffp-flow-active { stroke-width: 3; filter: url(#glow); animation: flowDash 0.8s linear infinite; }
         ```
       - Layout constants: `NODE_W=230, NODE_H=72, COL_GAP=380, ROW_GAP=120, MARGIN=60`
       - Edge routing uses layer-difference-aware control point pushing and 36px parallel spread

   - **`d:\Programming\Python\AlertProcessor\frontend\package.json`** — Read to confirm dependencies (Vue 3, Vite, no D3/ECharts installed)

   - **Arrow still invisible (Iteration 2)**: Root cause was `markerUnits="strokeWidth"` making markers scale to enormous sizes (effectively invisible/off-screen). Fixed by changing to `markerUnits="userSpaceOnUse"` with fixed `18x14` dimensions. Also changed marker IDs from token addresses (`#arrow-0xeee...`) to numeric indices (`#arr-0`) to avoid potential SVG ID matching issues with address characters.
   - **Build verification**: Ran `npx vite build` and `read_lints` after each change — both passed without errors.

构建通过 (93 modules, 0 errors)。问题已彻底修复。

**根本原因分析**:

之前悬浮节点不显示流动效果有 **2 个 bug**:

| Bug | 旧代码 | 问题 |
|-----|--------|------|
| **v-if 条件渲染** | `<path v-if="isEdgeActive(edge.id)">` | SVG 中动态增删带 `filter` 的路径存在渲染时序问题 — 路径可能不显示 |
| **opacity 倒置** | 激活态 `opacity: 0.25`，未激活 `0.65` | 悬浮后边反而变暗到几乎不可见 |

**修复方案** (模板 + CSS 双重改动):

1. **移除 `v-if`** → 改用 CSS `opacity: 0→1` 过渡
   - 动画路径始终存在于 DOM 中，通过 `.ffp-flow-visible` class 控制显隐
   ```html
   <path class="ffp-flow-line" :class="{ 'ffp-flow-visible': isEdgeActive(edge.id), 'ffp-flow-reverse': edge.isReverse }" />
   ```

2. **修正视觉层级**
   - 默认边: `opacity:.6`
   - **激活边**: `opacity:1` + `stroke-width:2.5` + `filter:url(#glow)` — 更亮更粗带发光
   - 非激活被淡化: `opacity:.12`

3. **动画默认暂停，激活时启动**
   ```css
   .ffp-flow-line { opacity:0; animation:none; }        /* 隐藏+静止 */
   .ffp-flow-visible { opacity:1; animation:flowDash .8s linear infinite; } /* 显示+流动 */
   ```

现在悬浮紫色节点时：连接该节点的所有边会高亮加粗发光 + 显示流动粒子动画，方向与箭头一致（左→右 或 右→左），其余非关联边自动淡化。

{"type":"reasoning","text":"用户想知道不同代币的 decimals 数据来源，不需要修改文件。根据之前 code-explorer 的分析结果，我已经掌握了完整的信息。让我直接回答。"}

根据代码探索结果，decimals 数据来源有 **3 层优先级**：

---

### 1. ETH 原生代币 — 硬编码

**文件**: `detectors/trace/analyzer.py:676-678`

```python
if not tok_addr:
    symbol = "ETH"
    decimals = 18          # ← 固定 18
    formatted = _format_eth_change(net_amount)
```

ETH 走独立路径 `_format_eth_change()`，内部硬编码 `divisor = 1e18`。

---

### 2. 已注册代币 — 协议注册表查询

**文件**: `detectors/trace/protocol_registry.py:117-201`

```python
_TOKEN_SYMBOLS: dict[str, dict] = {
    "0xc02aaa39b223fe8d0a0e5c4f27ead9083c756cc2": {"symbol": "WETH", "decimals": 18},
    "0xbb4cdb9cbd36b01bd1cbaebf2de08d9173bc095c":     {"symbol": "WBNB", "decimals": 18},
    "0x55d398326f99059ff775485246999027b3197955":       {"symbol": "USDT", "decimals": 18},
    "0x2170ed0880ac9a755fd29b2688956bd959f933f8":       {"symbol": "WETH", "decimals": 18},
    "0x8ac76a51cc950d9822d68b83fe1ad97b32cd580d":       {"symbol": "USDC", "decimals": 18},
    "0xe9e7cea3dedca5984780bafc599bd69add087d56":       {"symbol": "BUSD", "decimals": 18},
}
```

通过 `get_token_decimals(address)` 方法查找：
```python
def get_token_decimals(self, address: str) -> int:
    addr = address.lower().strip()
    tok = _TOKEN_SYMBOLS.get(addr)
    return tok["decimals"] if tok else 18   # ← 未找到默认 18
```

**当前注册表仅覆盖 6 个合约地址**（主要是 BSC 链 + ETH 主网 WETH）。

---

### 3. 未注册/未知代币 — 默认值兜底

**文件**: `detectors/trace/analyzer.py:684-687`

```python
else:
    info = token_info.get(tok_addr, {})
    decimals = info.get("decimals", 18)       # ← ★ 查不到就默认 18
    divisor = 10 ** decimals
    formatted = f"{net_amount / divisor:.4f}"
```

其中 `token_info` 来自 `protocol_registry.get_all_token_info()` 或调用方传入的 token 信息字典。如果该代币既不在 `_TOKEN_SYMBOLS` 注册表中，也不在传入的 `token_info` 里，**统一按 decimals=18 处理**。

---

### 完整决策链路

| 代币类型 | decimals 来源 | 具体位置 |
|---------|-------------|---------|
| **ETH** (token_addr=`""`) | 硬编码 `18` | `analyzer.py:677` |
| **已注册 ERC20/WBNB 等** | `_TOKEN_SYMBOLS` 字典 | `protocol_registry.py:117-126` |
| **传入的 token_info 中有的** | 调用方提供的 dict | `analyzer.py:684` via `token_info.get()` |
| **完全未知的代币** | **默认 `18`** | `analyzer.py:684` fallback |

### ⚠️ 潜在风险

当前注册表覆盖面很窄（仅 6 个地址），且**所有未识别代币一律按 18 位精度处理**。如果遇到 USDC（实际是 6 位）或其他非标准精度的代币且不在注册表中，余额计算会偏大 \(10^{(18-实际decimals)}\) 倍。
